this.$ = this.jQuery = jQuery.noConflict(true);

(function() {
    'use strict';

    // ===== 彻底屏蔽"离开此网站"原生弹框 =====
    // 浏览器原生 beforeunload 弹框无法被脚本自动点击，只能从源头阻止其注册
    (function blockLeaveDialog() {
        // 1) 拦截 EventTarget 原型上的 addEventListener（覆盖 window/document 等所有对象）
        var _etAdd = EventTarget.prototype.addEventListener;
        EventTarget.prototype.addEventListener = function(type, listener, options) {
            if (type === 'beforeunload' || type === 'unload') {
                return; // 直接丢弃，不注册
            }
            return _etAdd.apply(this, arguments);
        };
        // 2) 重定义 window/document 的 onbeforeunload 属性，赋值无效且永远返回 null
        [window, document].forEach(function(obj) {
            try {
                delete obj.onbeforeunload;
                Object.defineProperty(obj, 'onbeforeunload', {
                    set: function() {},
                    get: function() { return null; },
                    configurable: true
                });
            } catch (e) {}
        });
        // 3) 兜底：周期性清空（防止某些框架动态重新赋值）
        setInterval(function() {
            try { window.onbeforeunload = null; document.onbeforeunload = null; } catch (e) {}
        }, 300);
    })();

    // 默认配置
    var defaultConfig = {
        delayTime: 10,
        maxChapters: 0, // 0表示全部章节
        maxExercises: 0, // 0表示全部练习
        autoStart: false
    };

    var answerTitlejson = {};

    // 设置默认密码
    $("[name='password']").val("xfks1234");

    // 完整的答案数据（这里只保留部分作为示例）
    var totalAnswer = {
        "18287": "D", "18292": "B", "18281": "A", "18278": "A", "18280": "B",

    };

    var courseList = [];
    var chapterList = [];
    var exerciseList = [];
    var courseCount = 0;
    var chapterCount = 0;
    var exerciseCount = 0;

    // 使用GM存储来持久化状态变量
    var isLearning = GM_getValue("isLearning", false);
    var isExercising = GM_getValue("isExercising", false);
    var isOneKeyLearning = GM_getValue("isOneKeyLearning", false);
    var currentChapterIndex = GM_getValue("currentChapterIndex", 0);
    var currentExerciseIndex = GM_getValue("currentExerciseIndex", 0);
    var totalChapters = GM_getValue("totalChapters", 0);
    var totalExercises = GM_getValue("totalExercises", 0);

    // 初始化配置
    function initConfig() {
        var config = GM_getValue("config", null);
        if (!config) {
            config = defaultConfig;
            GM_setValue("config", config);
        }
        return config;
    }

    // 保存配置
    function saveConfig(config) {
        GM_setValue("config", config);
    }

    // 保存状态变量
    function saveState() {
        GM_setValue("isLearning", isLearning);
        GM_setValue("isExercising", isExercising);
        GM_setValue("isOneKeyLearning", isOneKeyLearning);
        GM_setValue("currentChapterIndex", currentChapterIndex);
        GM_setValue("currentExerciseIndex", currentExerciseIndex);
        GM_setValue("totalChapters", totalChapters);
        GM_setValue("totalExercises", totalExercises);
    }

    // 获取题库数量
    function getQuestionBankCount() {
        var answerTitlejson = GM_getValue("AnswerForTitle", defaultAnswerTitlejson);
        var count = 0;
        for (var key in answerTitlejson) {
            if (answerTitlejson.hasOwnProperty(key)) {
                count++;
            }
        }
        return count;
    }

    // 创建配置界面
    function createConfigPanel() {
        var config = initConfig();

        // 创建浮动面板
        var panel = $('<div id="xuefa-config-panel" style="position: fixed; top: 50px; right: 20px; width: 350px; background: white; border: 2px solid #4CAF50; border-radius: 10px; box-shadow: 0 4px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.2); z-index: 10000; font-family: Arial, sans-serif; display: none;">');

        var header = $('<div style="background: #4CAF50; color: white; padding: 10px; border-radius: 8px 8px 0 0; cursor: move; font-weight: bold; text-align: center;">学习配置面板</div>');
        var content = $('<div style="padding: 15px;">');

        // 学习间隔配置
        content.append('<label style="display: block; margin-bottom: 5px; font-weight: bold;">学习间隔(毫秒):</label>');
        var delayInput = $('<input type="number" min="100" max="60000" step="100" style="width: 100%; padding: 5px; margin-bottom: 10px; border: 1px solid #ddd; border-radius: 4px;">').val(config.delayTime);
        content.append(delayInput);

        // 最大章节数配置
        content.append('<label style="display: block; margin-bottom: 5px; font-weight: bold;">最大章节数(0=全部):</label>');
        var chaptersInput = $('<input type="number" min="0" max="100" style="width: 100%; padding: 5px; margin-bottom: 10px; border: 1px solid #ddd; border-radius: 4px;">').val(config.maxChapters);
        content.append(chaptersInput);

        // 最大练习数配置
        content.append('<label style="display: block; margin-bottom: 5px; font-weight: bold;">最大练习数(0=全部):</label>');
        var exercisesInput = $('<input type="number" min="0" max="100" style="width: 100%; padding: 5px; margin-bottom: 10px; border: 1px solid #ddd; border-radius: 4px;">').val(config.maxExercises);
        content.append(exercisesInput);

        // 进度显示区域
        var progressArea = $('<div id="xuefa-progress-area" style="margin: 15px 0; padding: 10px; background: #f9f9f9; border-radius: 5px; border: 1px solid #e0e0e0;">');

        // 题库数量显示
        var questionBankCount = getQuestionBankCount();
        var questionBankHtml = `
            <div style="margin-bottom: 10px; padding: 8px; background: #E3F2FD; border-radius: 5px;">
                <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center;">
                    <span style="font-weight: bold; color: #1976D2;">题库数量:</span>
                    <span id="question-bank-count" style="font-weight: bold; color: #1976D2; font-size: 16px;">${questionBankCount}</span>
                </div>
                <div style="font-size: 11px; color: #666; margin-top: 5px;">题目-答案对应关系数量</div>
            </div>
        `;

        // 章节进度
        var chapterProgressHtml = `
            <div style="margin-bottom: 10px;">
                <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin-bottom: 5px;">
                    <span style="font-weight: bold;">章节进度:</span>
                    <span id="chapter-progress-text">${currentChapterIndex}/${totalChapters}</span>
                </div>
                <div style="width: 100%; height: 20px; background: #e0e0e0; border-radius: 10px; overflow: hidden;">
                    <div id="chapter-progress-bar" style="height: 100%; background: #4CAF50; width: ${totalChapters > 0 ? (currentChapterIndex / totalChapters * 100).toFixed(1) : 0}%; transition: width 0.3s; text-align: center; color: white; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20px;">${totalChapters > 0 ? (currentChapterIndex / totalChapters * 100).toFixed(1) : 0}%</div>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;

        // 练习进度
        var exerciseProgressHtml = `
            <div>
                <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin-bottom: 5px;">
                    <span style="font-weight: bold;">练习进度:</span>
                    <span id="exercise-progress-text">${currentExerciseIndex}/${totalExercises}</span>
                </div>
                <div style="width: 100%; height: 20px; background: #e0e0e0; border-radius: 10px; overflow: hidden;">
                    <div id="exercise-progress-bar" style="height: 100%; background: #2196F3; width: ${totalExercises > 0 ? (currentExerciseIndex / totalExercises * 100).toFixed(1) : 0}%; transition: width 0.3s; text-align: center; color: white; font-size: 12px; line-height: 20px;">${totalExercises > 0 ? (currentExerciseIndex / totalExercises * 100).toFixed(1) : 0}%</div>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;

        progressArea.append(questionBankHtml).append(chapterProgressHtml).append(exerciseProgressHtml);
        content.append(progressArea);

        // 按钮区域
        var buttonArea = $('<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; margin-top: 15px;">');

        // 一键学习+练习按钮
        var oneKeyBtn = $('<button id="one-key-btn" style="background: #FF5722; color: white; border: none; padding: 10px 16px; margin: 0 5px; border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; margin-bottom: 10px; font-weight: bold; width: 80%;">一键学习+练习</button>');
        var pauseOneKeyBtn = $('<button id="pause-one-key-btn" style="background: #FF9800; color: white; border: none; padding: 10px 16px; margin: 0 5px; border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; margin-bottom: 10px; display: none; font-weight: bold; width: 80%;">暂停一键学习</button>');
        var stopOneKeyBtn = $('<button id="stop-one-key-btn" style="background: #f44336; color: white; border: none; padding: 10px 16px; margin: 0 5px; border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; margin-bottom: 10px; display: none; font-weight: bold; width: 80%;">停止一键学习</button>');

        // 学习章节按钮
        var startLearnBtn = $('<button id="start-learn-btn" style="background: #4CAF50; color: white; border: none; padding: 8px 16px; margin: 0 5px; border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; margin-bottom: 5px;">开始学习章节</button>');
        var pauseLearnBtn = $('<button id="pause-learn-btn" style="background: #FF9800; color: white; border: none; padding: 8px 16px; margin: 0 5px; border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; margin-bottom: 5px; display: none;">暂停学习</button>');
        var stopLearnBtn = $('<button id="stop-learn-btn" style="background: #f44336; color: white; border: none; padding: 8px 16px; margin: 0 5px; border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; margin-bottom: 5px; display: none;">停止学习</button>');

        // 练习按钮
        var startExerciseBtn = $('<button id="start-exercise-btn" style="background: #2196F3; color: white; border: none; padding: 8px 16px; margin: 0 5px; border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; margin-bottom: 5px;">开始练习</button>');
        var pauseExerciseBtn = $('<button id="pause-exercise-btn" style="background: #FF9800; color: white; border: none; padding: 8px 16px; margin: 0 5px; border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; margin-bottom: 5px; display: none;">暂停练习</button>');
        var stopExerciseBtn = $('<button id="stop-exercise-btn" style="background: #f44336; color: white; border: none; padding: 8px 16px; margin: 0 5px; border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; margin-bottom: 5px; display: none;">停止练习</button>');

        // 通用按钮
        var saveBtn = $('<button style="background: #9C27B0; color: white; border: none; padding: 8px 16px; margin: 0 5px; border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; margin-bottom: 5px;">保存配置</button>');
        var closeBtn = $('<button style="background: #9E9E9E; color: white; border: none; padding: 8px 16px; margin: 0 5px; border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; margin-bottom: 5px;">关闭</button>');
        var refreshQuestionBankBtn = $('<button id="refresh-question-bank-btn" style="background: #FF9800; color: white; border: none; padding: 8px 16px; margin: 0 5px; border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; margin-bottom: 5px;">刷新题库</button>');

        buttonArea.append(oneKeyBtn).append(pauseOneKeyBtn).append(stopOneKeyBtn)
                 .append($('<div style="margin: 10px 0; border-top: 1px dashed #ccc;"></div>'))
                 .append(startLearnBtn).append(pauseLearnBtn).append(stopLearnBtn)
                 .append($('<div style="margin: 10px 0; border-top: 1px dashed #ccc;"></div>'))
                 .append(startExerciseBtn).append(pauseExerciseBtn).append(stopExerciseBtn)
                 .append($('<div style="margin: 10px 0; border-top: 1px dashed #ccc;"></div>'))
                 .append(saveBtn).append(refreshQuestionBankBtn).append(closeBtn);
        content.append(buttonArea);

        // 状态显示
        var statusDiv = $('<div id="xuefa-status" style="margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px; background: #f5f5f5; border-radius: 4px; font-size: 12px; display: none;"></div>');
        content.append(statusDiv);

        panel.append(header).append(content);
        $('body').append(panel);

        // 让面板内所有按钮铺满且不溢出
        $('#xuefa-config-panel button').css({
            'width': '100%',
            'box-sizing': 'border-box',
            'margin': '0'
        });

        // 创建悬浮按钮
        var floatBtn = $('<div id="xuefa-float-btn" style="position: fixed; top: 20px; right: 20px; width: 50px; height: 50px; background: #4CAF50; color: white; border-radius: 50%; text-align: center; line-height: 50px; cursor: pointer; box-shadow: 0 2px 5px rgba(0,0,0,0.3); z-index: 9999; font-weight: bold; font-size: 20px;">学</div>');
        $('body').append(floatBtn);

        // 初始化按钮状态
        updateLearnButtonState();
        updateExerciseButtonState();
        updateOneKeyButtonState();

        // 事件绑定
        floatBtn.click(function() {
            panel.toggle();
            updateProgressDisplay();
            updateQuestionBankCount();
        });

        oneKeyBtn.click(function() {
            if (!isOneKeyLearning) {
                startOneKeyLearning();
            } else {
                updateStatus("一键学习正在进行中...");
            }
        });

        pauseOneKeyBtn.click(function() {
            pauseOneKeyLearning();
        });

        stopOneKeyBtn.click(function() {
            stopOneKeyLearning();
        });

        startLearnBtn.click(function() {
            if (!isLearning) {
                startLearning();
            } else {
                updateStatus("学习正在进行中...");
            }
        });

        pauseLearnBtn.click(function() {
            pauseLearning();
        });

        stopLearnBtn.click(function() {
            stopLearning();
        });

        startExerciseBtn.click(function() {
            if (!isExercising) {
                startExercising();
            } else {
                updateStatus("练习正在进行中...");
            }
        });

        pauseExerciseBtn.click(function() {
            pauseExercising();
        });

        stopExerciseBtn.click(function() {
            stopExercising();
        });

        saveBtn.click(function() {
            var newConfig = {
                delayTime: parseInt(delayInput.val()),
                maxChapters: parseInt(chaptersInput.val()),
                maxExercises: parseInt(exercisesInput.val()),
                autoStart: false // 固定为false，不再使用自动开始
            };
            saveConfig(newConfig);
            updateStatus("配置已保存!");
        });

        refreshQuestionBankBtn.click(function() {
            updateQuestionBankCount();
            updateStatus("题库数量已刷新!");
        });

        closeBtn.click(function() {
            panel.hide();
        });

        // 拖拽功能
        var isDragging = false;
        var dragOffset = {x: 0, y: 0};

        header.mousedown(function(e) {
            isDragging = true;
            var offset = panel.offset();
            dragOffset.x = e.clientX - offset.left;
            dragOffset.y = e.clientY - offset.top;
            return false;
        });

        $(document).mousemove(function(e) {
            if (isDragging) {
                panel.css({
                    left: (e.clientX - dragOffset.x) + 'px',
                    top: (e.clientY - dragOffset.y) + 'px'
                });
            }
        });

        $(document).mouseup(function() {
            isDragging = false;
        });
    }

    // 更新题库数量显示
    function updateQuestionBankCount() {
        var count = getQuestionBankCount();
        $('#question-bank-count').text(count);
    }

    // 练习页面悬浮显示：题库数量 + 当前练习进度
    function createExerciseHUD() {
        if ($('#xuefa-exercise-hud').length > 0) return;
        var hud = $('<div id="xuefa-exercise-hud" style="position: fixed; left: 20px; top: 20px; z-index: 10001; background: rgba(33,150,243,0.95); color: #fff; padding: 10px 14px; border-radius: 8px; box-shadow: 0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.3); font-family: Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 13px; line-height: 1.7; min-width: 170px;">');
        hud.append('<div style="font-weight:bold; margin-bottom:6px; border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.4); padding-bottom:4px;">练习进度</div>');
        hud.append('<div>题库数量：<span id="hud-qb-count" style="font-weight:bold;">0</span></div>');
        hud.append('<div>完成进度：<span id="hud-ex-progress" style="font-weight:bold;">0 / 0</span></div>');
        hud.append('<div style="height:8px; background:rgba(255,255,255,0.3); border-radius:4px; margin-top:6px; overflow:hidden;"><div id="hud-ex-bar" style="height:100%; width:0%; background:#fff; transition:width 0.3s;"></div></div>');
        $('body').append(hud);
        updateExerciseHUD();
    }

    function updateExerciseHUD() {
        var hud = $('#xuefa-exercise-hud');
        if (hud.length === 0) return;
        var count = getQuestionBankCount();
        var exList = GM_getValue('exerciseList', []);
        var total = exList ? exList.length : 0;
        var cur = GM_getValue('currentExerciseIndex', 0);
        var shownCur = total > 0 ? Math.min(cur + 1, total) : 0;
        $('#hud-qb-count').text(count);
        $('#hud-ex-progress').text(shownCur + ' / ' + total);
        var pct = total > 0 ? (shownCur / total * 100).toFixed(1) : 0;
        $('#hud-ex-bar').css('width', pct + '%');
    }

    // 在考试/练习界面上勾选已找到答案的选项（answer 形如 A / AB / ABCD / V / X）
    function selectAnswerInUI(qitem, answer) {
        qitem.find("input[type='radio'], input[type='checkbox']").each(function() {
            var v = $(this).val();
            if (answer.indexOf(v) >= 0) {
                $(this).prop("checked", true);
                try {
                    this.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
                } catch (e) {}
            }
        });
        // 标记已成功选中（浅绿底），区别于未找到答案的灰色
        qitem.css("background", "#e8f5e9");
    }

    // 更新进度显示
    function updateProgressDisplay() {
        var chapterProgress = totalChapters > 0 ? (currentChapterIndex / totalChapters * 100).toFixed(1) : 0;
        var exerciseProgress = totalExercises > 0 ? (currentExerciseIndex / totalExercises * 100).toFixed(1) : 0;

        $('#chapter-progress-bar').css('width', chapterProgress + '%').text(chapterProgress + '%');
        $('#chapter-progress-text').text(currentChapterIndex + '/' + totalChapters);

        $('#exercise-progress-bar').css('width', exerciseProgress + '%').text(exerciseProgress + '%');
        $('#exercise-progress-text').text(currentExerciseIndex + '/' + totalExercises);

        // 保存状态
        saveState();

        // 更新题库数量
        updateQuestionBankCount();
    }

    // 更新状态显示
    function updateStatus(message) {
        var statusDiv = $('#xuefa-status');
        statusDiv.text(message).show();
        setTimeout(function() {
            statusDiv.fadeOut();
        }, 200);
    }

    // 开始一键学习+练习
    function startOneKeyLearning() {
        $('#xuefa-config-panel').show();
        if (isOneKeyLearning || isLearning || isExercising) {
            updateStatus("已有任务正在进行中...");
            return;
        }
        isOneKeyLearning = true;
        saveState();
        updateOneKeyButtonState();
        updateStatus("开始一键学习+练习...");

        // 重置进度
        currentChapterIndex = 0;
        currentExerciseIndex = 0;
        saveState();

        var config = initConfig();
        GM_setValue("delaytime", config.delayTime);

        // 检测课程列表
        FindCourseList();

        // 根据配置限制章节和练习数量
        if (config.maxChapters > 0 && chapterList.length > config.maxChapters) {
            chapterList = chapterList.slice(0, config.maxChapters);
        }
        if (config.maxExercises > 0 && exerciseList.length > config.maxExercises) {
            exerciseList = exerciseList.slice(0, config.maxExercises);
        }

        totalChapters = chapterList.length;
        totalExercises = exerciseList.length;
        saveState();

        updateProgressDisplay();
        updateStatus(`开始一键学习 ${totalChapters} 个章节和 ${totalExercises} 份练习题`);

        // 开始学习章节
        isLearning = true;
        saveState();
        updateLearnButtonState();

        StartLearnAll();
    }

    // 暂停一键学习
    function pauseOneKeyLearning() {
        isOneKeyLearning = false;
        isLearning = false;
        isExercising = false;
        saveState();
        updateOneKeyButtonState();
        updateLearnButtonState();
        updateExerciseButtonState();
        updateStatus("一键学习已暂停");
    }

    // 停止一键学习
    function stopOneKeyLearning() {
        isOneKeyLearning = false;
        isLearning = false;
        isExercising = false;
        currentChapterIndex = 0;
        currentExerciseIndex = 0;
        saveState();
        updateOneKeyButtonState();
        updateLearnButtonState();
        updateExerciseButtonState();
        updateProgressDisplay();
        updateStatus("一键学习已停止");
    }

    // 开始学习章节
    function startLearning() {
        $('#xuefa-config-panel').show();
        if (isLearning) {
            updateStatus("学习正在进行中...");
            return;
        }

        isLearning = true;
        saveState();
        updateLearnButtonState();
        updateStatus("开始检测课程...");

        // 重置进度
        currentChapterIndex = 0;
        saveState();

        var config = initConfig();
        GM_setValue("delaytime", config.delayTime);

        // 检测课程列表
        FindCourseList();

        // 根据配置限制章节数量
        if (config.maxChapters > 0 && chapterList.length > config.maxChapters) {
            chapterList = chapterList.slice(0, config.maxChapters);
        }

        totalChapters = chapterList.length;
        saveState();

        updateProgressDisplay();
        updateStatus(`开始学习 ${totalChapters} 个章节`);

        // 开始学习章节
        StartLearnAll();
    }

    // 暂停学习
    function pauseLearning() {
        isLearning = false;
        saveState();
        updateLearnButtonState();
        updateStatus("学习已暂停");
    }

    // 停止学习
    function stopLearning() {
        isLearning = false;
        currentChapterIndex = 0;
        saveState();
        updateLearnButtonState();
        updateProgressDisplay();
        updateStatus("学习已停止");
    }

    // 开始练习
    function startExercising() {
        $('#xuefa-config-panel').show();
        if (isExercising) {
            updateStatus("练习正在进行中...");
            return;
        }

        isExercising = true;
        saveState();
        updateExerciseButtonState();
        updateStatus("开始检测练习题...");

        // 重置进度
        currentExerciseIndex = 0;
        saveState();

        var config = initConfig();

        // 检测课程列表
        FindCourseList();

        // 根据配置限制练习数量
        if (config.maxExercises > 0 && exerciseList.length > config.maxExercises) {
            exerciseList = exerciseList.slice(0, config.maxExercises);
        }

        totalExercises = exerciseList.length;
        saveState();

        updateProgressDisplay();
        updateStatus(`开始练习 ${totalExercises} 份练习题`);

        // 开始练习
        StartExercise();
    }

    // 暂停练习
    function pauseExercising() {
        isExercising = false;
        saveState();
        updateExerciseButtonState();
        updateStatus("练习已暂停");
    }

    // 停止练习
    function stopExercising() {
        isExercising = false;
        currentExerciseIndex = 0;
        saveState();
        updateExerciseButtonState();
        updateProgressDisplay();
        updateStatus("练习已停止");
    }

    // 更新一键学习按钮状态
    function updateOneKeyButtonState() {
        if (isOneKeyLearning) {
            $('#one-key-btn').hide();
            $('#pause-one-key-btn').show();
            $('#stop-one-key-btn').show();
        } else {
            $('#one-key-btn').show();
            $('#pause-one-key-btn').hide();
            $('#stop-one-key-btn').hide();
        }
    }

    // 更新学习按钮状态
    function updateLearnButtonState() {
        if (isLearning) {
            $('#start-learn-btn').hide();
            $('#pause-learn-btn').show();
            $('#stop-learn-btn').show();
        } else {
            $('#start-learn-btn').show();
            $('#pause-learn-btn').hide();
            $('#stop-learn-btn').hide();
        }
    }

    // 更新练习按钮状态
    function updateExerciseButtonState() {
        if (isExercising) {
            $('#start-exercise-btn').hide();
            $('#pause-exercise-btn').show();
            $('#stop-exercise-btn').show();
        } else {
            $('#start-exercise-btn').show();
            $('#pause-exercise-btn').hide();
            $('#stop-exercise-btn').hide();
        }
    }

    // 检测课程列表
    function FindCourseList() {
        var indexdom;
        $.ajax({
            type: "get",
            url: "https://xfks-study.gdsf.gov.cn/study/index",
            data: null,
            async: false,
            success: function(data) {
                indexdom = $(data).find("#pre");
            }
        });

        updateStatus("开始检测课程");
        console.log("开始检测课程");
        courseList = [];
        chapterList = [];
        exerciseList = [];
        courseCount = 0;
        chapterCount = 0;
        exerciseCount = 0;

        indexdom.find(".card.current").each(function() {
            courseList.push($(this).children("h3").children("a").attr("href"));
            courseCount++;
        });
        console.log("检测到" + courseCount + "个课程");
        console.log("开始检测章节及练习题");
        for (let i = 0; i < courseList.length; i++) {
            var percentnum = ((i + 1) / courseList.length * 100).toFixed(0);
            FindChapterList(courseList[i]);
        }
        updateStatus("检测到" + chapterCount + "个章节," + exerciseCount + "份练习题");
        console.log("检测到" + chapterCount + "个章节," + exerciseCount + "份练习题。");
        GM_setValue("chapterList", chapterList);
        GM_setValue("exerciseList", exerciseList);
    }

    function FindChapterList(courseUrl) {
        var chaptersdom;
        var rootdom;
        $.ajax({
            type: "get",
            url: courseUrl,
            data: null,
            async: false,
            success: function(data) {
                rootdom = $(data);
                chaptersdom = $(data).find("#chapter-none");
            }
        });
        chaptersdom.children("li").each(function() {
            if ($(this).find("a").attr("href").indexOf("study/course") > 0) {
                chapterList.push("https://xfks-study.gdsf.gov.cn" + $(this).find("a").attr("href"));
                chapterCount++;
            }
        })
        rootdom.find("td.title").find("a[onclick]").each(function() {
            var onclickString = $(this).attr("onclick");
            var courseIdString = onclickString.split(',')[0].split('(')[1];
            var chapterIdString = onclickString.split(',')[1].split(')')[0];
            exerciseList.push({
                'courseId': courseIdString,
                'chapterId': chapterIdString
            });
            exerciseCount++;
        });
    }

    function StartLearnAll() {
        var chapterList = GM_getValue("chapterList", null);
        var config = initConfig();

        function learnNextChapter() {
            if (!isLearning || currentChapterIndex >= chapterList.length) {
                if (isLearning) {
                    updateStatus("章节学习完成!");
                    isLearning = false;
                    saveState();
                    updateLearnButtonState();

                    // 如果是一键学习模式，自动开始练习
                    if (isOneKeyLearning) {
                        updateStatus("章节学习完成，开始练习...");
                        isExercising = true;
                        saveState();
                        updateExerciseButtonState();
                        StartExercise();
                    }
                }
                return;
            }

            // 如果设置了最大章节数并且已经达到，则停止
            if (config.maxChapters > 0 && currentChapterIndex >= config.maxChapters) {
                updateStatus("已达到最大章节数");
                isLearning = false;
                saveState();
                updateLearnButtonState();

                // 如果是一键学习模式，自动开始练习
                if (isOneKeyLearning) {
                    updateStatus("已达到最大章节数，开始练习...");
                    isExercising = true;
                    saveState();
                    updateExerciseButtonState();
                    StartExercise();
                }
                return;
            }

            var chapterUrl = chapterList[currentChapterIndex];
            var getchaptersuccess = getChapterContent(chapterUrl);
            if (getchaptersuccess) {
                var indexchapter = chapterUrl.indexOf("chapter");
                var chapternum = chapterUrl.substring(indexchapter + 8);
                console.log('获取章节：' + chapternum + '成功，进度：' + (currentChapterIndex + 1) + "/" + chapterList.length);

                var delaytimenum = Math.round(Math.random() * 10);
                var delaytime = GM_getValue("delaytime", config.delayTime);

                setTimeout(function() {
                    submitLearn(chapternum);
                    currentChapterIndex++;
                    saveState();
                    updateProgressDisplay();
                    updateStatus(`学习进度: ${currentChapterIndex}/${chapterList.length} 章节`);

                    // 学习下一个章节
                    learnNextChapter();
                }, delaytimenum * delaytime);
            } else {
                // 如果获取章节内容失败，跳过这个章节
                currentChapterIndex++;
                saveState();
                learnNextChapter();
            }
        }

        // 开始学习第一个章节
        learnNextChapter();
    }

    function StartExercise() {
        var exerciseList = GM_getValue("exerciseList", null);
        var config = initConfig();

        // 如果设置了最大练习数，则只做指定数量的练习
        if (config.maxExercises > 0 && exerciseList.length > config.maxExercises) {
            exerciseList = exerciseList.slice(0, config.maxExercises);
        }

        if (exerciseList.length > 0 && isExercising) {
            currentExerciseIndex = 0;
            saveState();
            updateProgressDisplay();
            // 开始第一个练习
            exerciseBuild(exerciseList[0]['courseId'], exerciseList[0]['chapterId']);
        } else {
            updateStatus("没有找到练习题!");
            isExercising = false;
            if (isOneKeyLearning) {
                isOneKeyLearning = false;
                updateStatus("一键学习+练习完成!");
            }
            saveState();
            updateExerciseButtonState();
            updateOneKeyButtonState();
        }
    }

    // 跳转到下一个练习
    function goToNextExercise() {
        // 从存储中重新获取状态，确保是最新的
        isExercising = GM_getValue("isExercising", false);
        isOneKeyLearning = GM_getValue("isOneKeyLearning", false);

        if (!isExercising) {
            return;
        }

        var exerciseList = GM_getValue("exerciseList", null);
        var config = initConfig();

        currentExerciseIndex++;
        saveState();
        updateProgressDisplay();
        updateExerciseHUD();

        // 如果设置了最大练习数并且已经达到，则停止
        if (config.maxExercises > 0 && currentExerciseIndex >= config.maxExercises) {
            updateStatus("已达到最大练习数");
            isExercising = false;
            if (isOneKeyLearning) {
                isOneKeyLearning = false;
                updateStatus("一键学习+练习完成!");
            }
            saveState();
            updateExerciseButtonState();
            updateOneKeyButtonState();
            window.location.href = "https://xfks-study.gdsf.gov.cn/study/statistics";
            return;
        }

        window.onbeforeunload = null;

        if (currentExerciseIndex < exerciseList.length) {
            updateStatus(`准备下一个练习: ${currentExerciseIndex + 1}/${exerciseList.length}`);
            // 延迟一段时间后开始下一个练习
            setTimeout(function() {
                exerciseBuild(exerciseList[currentExerciseIndex]['courseId'], exerciseList[currentExerciseIndex]['chapterId']);
            }, 500);
        } else {
            updateStatus("所有练习已完成!");
            isExercising = false;
            if (isOneKeyLearning) {
                isOneKeyLearning = false;
                updateStatus("一键学习+练习完成!");
            }
            saveState();
            updateExerciseButtonState();
            updateOneKeyButtonState();
        }
    }

    // 其他原有函数保持不变...
    function getChapterContent(chapterUrl) {
        var result = false;
        $.ajax({
            type: "get",
            url: chapterUrl,
            data: null,
            async: false,
            success: function(data) {
                result = true;
            }
        });
        return result;
    }

    function submitLearn(chapterNum) {
        $.ajax({
            type: "post",
            url: "/study/learn/" + chapterNum,
            data: null,
            async: false,
            success: function(data) {
                //console.log("章节:"+chapterNum+"学习完毕");
            }
        });
    }

    function exerciseBuild(courseId, chapterId) {
        jQuery.ajax({
            url: "/study/exerciseBuild/" + courseId + "/chapter/" + chapterId,
            method: "post",
            data: null,
            success: function(data) {
                window.location.href = "/study/exercise/" + data;
            },
            error: function(data) {
                if (data.status == 400) {
                    alert(data.responseText);
                } else {
                    alert("创建失败");
                }
                // 练习创建失败，继续下一个练习
                goToNextExercise();
            }
        })
    }

    // 自动处理离开页面确认对话框
    function autoHandleLeaveDialog() {
        // 重写确认对话框
        window.onbeforeunload = null;

        // 尝试直接覆盖确认函数
        if (window.confirm) {
            var originalConfirm = window.confirm;
            window.confirm = function(message) {
                if (message && message.indexOf("离开此网站") >= 0) {
                    console.log("自动确认离开页面对话框");
                    return true;
                }
                return originalConfirm.apply(this, arguments);
            };
        }

        // 监听可能出现的确认对话框
        $(document).on('click', 'button, a', function() {
            // 如果点击了可能触发离开确认的元素，预先处理
            setTimeout(function() {
                window.onbeforeunload = null;
            }, 100);
        });

        // 定期清除onbeforeunload
        setInterval(function() {
            window.onbeforeunload = null;
        }, 500);
    }

    function sleep(delay) {
        var start = (new Date()).getTime();
        while ((new Date()).getTime() - start < delay) {
            continue;
        }
    }

    // 注册菜单命令
    GM_registerMenuCommand("打开学习配置", function() {
        $('#xuefa-config-panel').show();
        updateProgressDisplay();
    });

    GM_registerMenuCommand("一键学习+练习", function() {
        startOneKeyLearning();
    });

    GM_registerMenuCommand("开始学习章节", function() {
        startLearning();
    });

    GM_registerMenuCommand("开始练习", function() {
        startExercising();
    });

    // 初始化
    $(document).ready(function() {
        createConfigPanel();

        // 恢复学习状态
        var savedChapterList = GM_getValue("chapterList", []);
        var savedExerciseList = GM_getValue("exerciseList", []);
        if (savedChapterList.length > 0) {
            totalChapters = savedChapterList.length;
        }
        if (savedExerciseList.length > 0) {
            totalExercises = savedExerciseList.length;
        }
        updateProgressDisplay();

        // 练习页面逻辑
        if (fullurl.indexOf("exercise") > 0) {
            // 从存储中获取状态
            isExercising = GM_getValue("isExercising", false);
            isOneKeyLearning = GM_getValue("isOneKeyLearning", false);

            // 设置自动处理离开页面对话框
            autoHandleLeaveDialog();

            // 练习页面显示题库数量与进度悬浮窗
            createExerciseHUD();

            var exerciseList = GM_getValue("exerciseList", null);
            var exercisenum = fullurl.substring(fullurl.indexOf("exercise") + 9);
            var xuefaexerciseurl = "/study/submitExercise/" + exercisenum;
            answerjson = {};
            answerTitlejson = GM_getValue("AnswerForTitle", defaultAnswerTitlejson);

            // 更新当前练习索引
            for (var i = 0; i < exerciseList.length; i++) {
                if (exercisenum == exerciseList[i]['chapterId']) {
                    currentExerciseIndex = i;
                    saveState();
                    updateExerciseHUD();
                    break;
                }
            }

            $(".item").each(function() {
                var qid = $(this).attr("qid");
                var answer = $(this).find(".answer").attr("val");
                var answerTitleTempStr = $(this).find(".question-title").html();
                var subStartIndex = answerTitleTempStr.indexOf(".");
                var subEndIndex = answerTitleTempStr.indexOf("<label");
                var answerTitleStr = answerTitleTempStr.substring(subStartIndex, subEndIndex).replace(/\s+/g, "");
                answerjson[qid] = answer;
                answerTitlejson[answerTitleStr] = answer;
            });
            GM_setValue("AnswerForTitle", answerTitlejson);

            // 更新题库数量显示
            updateQuestionBankCount();

            var config = initConfig();
            var delaytimenum = Math.round(Math.random() * 80 + 20);
            var delaytime = config.delayTime;

            // 更新进度显示
            updateProgressDisplay();

            // 只有处于练习状态时才自动提交
            if (isExercising) {
                // 自动提交练习
                setTimeout(function() {
                    $.ajax({
                        type: "post",
                        url: xuefaexerciseurl,
                        data: answerjson,
                        async: false,
                        success: function(data) {
                            if (data.toString().indexOf('满分') > 0) {
                                updateStatus("练习提交成功，准备下一个练习");
                                // 更新题库数量
                                updateQuestionBankCount();
                                updateExerciseHUD();
                                // 自动跳转到下一个练习
                                setTimeout(function() {
                                    goToNextExercise();
                                }, 500);
                            } else {
                                updateStatus("练习提交完成");
                                // 更新题库数量
                                updateQuestionBankCount();
                                updateExerciseHUD();
                                // 即使不是满分也继续下一个练习
                                setTimeout(function() {
                                    goToNextExercise();
                                }, 500);
                            }
                        },
                        error: function() {
                            updateStatus("练习提交失败");
                            // 即使失败也继续下一个练习
                            setTimeout(function() {
                                goToNextExercise();
                            }, 500);
                        }
                    });
                }, delaytimenum * delaytime);
            }
        }

        // 原有的考试页面逻辑保持不变
        if (fullurl.indexOf("exams") > 0) {
            // ... 原有考试页面逻辑
            var examnum = fullurl.substring(fullurl.indexOf("exams") + 6);
            var examurl = "/study/exams/" + examnum;
            var saveexamurl = "/study/saveAnswer/" + examnum;
            var allanserhasfind = false;
            answerjson = {};
            answerTitlejson = GM_getValue("AnswerForTitle", {});
            var answerCount = 0;
            for (var x in answerTitlejson) {
                answerCount++;
            }
            if (answerCount == 0) {
                alert("系统题库为空，请做一遍练习题，丰富题库！");
            }
            console.log(answerTitlejson);
            console.log("题库数量为：" + answerCount + "条。");
            var rightqidcount = 0;
            var wrongqidcount = 0;
            var AnswerString = "";
            $(".item").each(function() {
                var qitem = $(this);
                var qid = qitem.attr("qid");
                var answerTitleTempStr = qitem.find(".question-title").html();
                var subStartIndex = answerTitleTempStr.indexOf(".");
                var answerTitleStr = answerTitleTempStr.substring(subStartIndex).replace(/\s+/g, "");
                var answer = null;
                if (typeof totalAnswer[qid] !== 'undefined') {
                    answer = totalAnswer[qid];
                } else if (typeof answerTitlejson[answerTitleStr] !== 'undefined') {
                    answer = answerTitlejson[answerTitleStr];
                }
                if (answer === null) {
                    wrongqidcount++;
                    qitem.css("background", "#ccc");
                } else {
                    AnswerString += qid + "=" + answer + "&";
                    rightqidcount++;
                    // 在界面上呈现选择结果（勾选对应选项）
                    selectAnswerInUI(qitem, answer);
                }
            });
            if (wrongqidcount > 0) {
                allanserhasfind = false;
            } else {
                allanserhasfind = true;
            }

            AnswerString = AnswerString.substring(0, AnswerString.length - 1);
            jQuery.ajax({
                url: saveexamurl,
                method: "post",
                data: AnswerString,
                success: function(data, text, xhr) {
                    if (xhr.status == 200) {
                        alert("已找到" + rightqidcount + "条题目答案，并保存！\n共" + wrongqidcount + "条题目未找到答案，未找到答案的题目已变灰!\n按F5刷新本页面可见！");
                    } else {
                        alert(data);
                    }
                },
                error: function(data) {
                    if (data.status == 400) {
                        alert(data.responseText)
                    } else {
                        alert("提交失败");
                    }
                }
            })
            if (allanserhasfind) {
                var issubmitanswer = confirm("全部答案已找齐，是否自动提交答案？");
                if (issubmitanswer) {
                    jQuery.ajax({
                        url: examurl,
                        method: "post",
                        data: AnswerString,
                        success: function(data, text, xhr) {
                            if (xhr.status == 200) {
                                window.location.href = '/study/exam';
                            } else {
                                alert(data);
                            }
                        },
                        error: function(data) {
                            if (data.status == 400) {
                                alert(data.responseText)
                            } else {
                                alert("提交失败");
                            }
                        }
                    })
                }
            }
        }
    });
})();
